Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the optimization object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 274. The moduli space has dimension at most 407 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_274(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 274. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^407(S, F) is trivial. QED.
